### Q-090 断言先写后测：手写经典站点的钉键被误读为「无规则」

`classic.PreToolUse.checkArgument` 首测传 `{tool_name:'B'} vs {tool_name:'A'}` 得 `undefined`，我先写成「该站点无入参规则」；读 `Gn` 正文后才知道钉的是**工具调用信封**（`["tool","tool_use_id","agentId","consent",$shadowed]`），`tool_name` 根本不在其中。改用 `tool` 键即得原句。

处置：回归与编译驱动都改用 `tool` 键实测，并在注释里点名「不是 `tool_name`」；S-1 来源清单补该条。分类 S-1。

### Q-091 拷贝缺口检查器把嵌套局部声明计为已声明（W45-22 的 `a8` 漏报）

W45-22 的流式副本引用 `a8` 而没有任何块声明它，`node scripts/source-undeclared-scan.mjs src/plugin-chain-stream-source.ts` 却报 `unresolved 0`；`.audit/w45-22-compiled.mjs` 首次运行即抛 `ReferenceError: a8 is not defined`。原因在检查器自身：它把**任意深度**的 `VariableDeclarator`、函数名、**每个函数参数**与 `Property.value` 都并进一个文件级 `declared` 集合，压缩正文里短名到处复用，于是某函数内的 `let a8` 顶替了另一函数里的自由 `a8`。检查器是绿的，语义从未被测过。

处置：重写为作用域链（模块/函数/块）＋提升＋逐引用解析，并修两处同时存在的误报——class 声明/表达式原先完全不绑定（`He`/`Gde`/`Ye`/`Hht`/`bN` 被读成自由名），以及删除 `// @ts-nocheck` 整行导致报告行号相对磁盘文件偏移 1（改为原位注释掉）。改后重扫 24 份拷贝，找出三处真缺口并关掉：流式副本 `ep`/`z3e`（仅在阶段 `.catch` 路径可达）、runner 副本 `x`（`WMt` 无 `Buffer` 分支）。回归 `test/source-undeclared-scan.test.ts` 11 夹具，其中 `a8` 形状是**旧语义必过**的那条。分类 S-7。

### Q-092 `it()` 每次返回新对象，写进注册表的 ui.log 订阅者被丢掉（W45-24）

`GCn`（ui.log 的订阅入口，chunk-sfn1dbxq.js [2338505,2338661)）在第一次调用即抛 `TypeError: r is not iterable`：它读 `it().pendingUiLog` 并把 `uiLogSink` 写在 `it()` 的返回对象上，而产品的 `it()` 每次调用都新建一个字面量（只含 `loadedModules`/`moduleSeats`/`hooksRespawns`/`registeredAgents`），写入即丢。源里 `it` 是 `function it(){return a().registry}`（chunk-3ph567jx.js [3729,3763)），注册表字面量在 [2322,2354) 处带 `uiLogSink:null,pendingUiLog:[]`，**同一个对象**被反复返回——一次调用绑定、下一次调用读取，正是这个身份让它成立。

处置：`src/plugin-chain-host.ts` 改为构建一个注册表对象并返回它，补上 `uiLogSink`/`pendingUiLog` 两个字段；四个既有字段不变。回归 `test/plugin-chain-ui.test.ts` 覆盖订阅（`GCn` 冲刷 `pendingUiLog` 且带 `again` 状态、解绑只清自己的 sink）。分类 S-7。

### Q-093 `src/tui-custom-theme-source.ts` 有 4 个未声明引用（作用域感知扫描发现，未跟踪文件）

作用域感知扫描（W45-23 改造后）在该文件报 `unresolved 4`：`gt`（第 20 行）、`Fe`/`Wi`/`Yf`（第 21 行，`P` 内的 `await Wi(...)` 等）。该文件未跟踪、无 `-blocks.json` 台账、也不在 `scripts/verify-chain-copies.mjs` 的计划表里，但被 `src/tui-custom-themes.ts` 导入，因此是运行期会抛的真实缺口（无测试驱动到这些行）。既有搬运纪律：先按使用方自己的 import map 认名（Q-089），从声明模块补块或扩导出，然后把这一对登记进校验器。

处置：立案，列为下一包（W45-25）。分类 S-7。
**已收束（W45-25，2026-10-15）**：四名各自认到声明模块后按整段搬运（`gt`+`i` chunk-9t9v3t5g.js [1879,2121)；`Yf`+`Wi` chunk-wdffj3z9.js [615,1006)；`Fe`+`r`/`f`/`h` chunk-nv1qvpv3.js [1614,4480)），三段均在 `src/tui-custom-theme-source.ts` 内逐字落位，扫描 `unresolved 0`。回归 `test/tui-custom-theme-reader.test.ts` 2/2 驱动 `load(reader)`：分页批量读（`Fe` 键形状）、`gt` 的 `EACCES fs: inner` 文案、`Yf` 的第 10000 页上限、reader 抛出与无 reader 两条旁路。台账 `research/ports/W44-custom-theme-copies.json` 增补三条，并新增可复跑校验 `node .audit/w45-25-splice.mjs --verify`（14 条记录，模块摘要 + 文件内逐字节，`bad 0`）。


### Q-094 三个夹具把子进程自身的启动算进了请求/启动预算，宿主有负载时夹具根本没起来

现象：`test/lsp.test.ts` 15/16（`timeouts cancel requests…` 卡在 3 s 的 `waitFor`）、`test/mcp-progress.test.ts` 7/8（`MCP request "initialize" … timed out after 90ms`）、`scripts/smoke-magpie-startup.mjs` 的 `cancellation()` 100 ms 用例（`starter PID marker missing: 8-deadline-100.pid`）。三条都在本包之前的提交态复现（detached worktree `dca9c20`：lsp 15/16、mcp-progress 7/8），三个文件与 launcher 对 HEAD 均 0 diff。

原因：这些预算同时覆盖夹具自身的启动与握手。`requestTimeoutMs` 是 LSP/MCP 客户端所有请求的时限（`src/lsp.ts` 的 `request()` 用它给 `initialize` 计时），`--startup-timeout-ms` 覆盖 launcher 拉起 starter 的全过程，而本机负载下**裸 `node` 启动实测 150-170 ms**（`/usr/bin/time node -e ""`，load 17-29），于是 `90/150/200 ms` 的预算落在启动成本之下：握手超时、子进程被回收，夹具一行都没写出来（临时探针 dump 出 `PROBE []`，请求日志为空；`/timed out/` 这条断言两种超时都能匹配，所以先前看不出是初始化超时）。排除"子进程被饿死"：同一个探针里"读一行→追加文件"的子进程在 load 39-77 下 11-12 ms 就回话（`.audit/w45-b1-probe.mjs`）。

处置：① `test/lsp.test.ts`：`waitFor` 默认 3 s → 15 s（附原因），夹具预算 150/150/200 → 2000；② `test/mcp-progress.test.ts`：`STDIO` 参数化为 `stdio(reports, stepMs)`，新增永不回话的 `stdioLoop`，预算按 `step < idle < total` 缩放（step 200，idle 500/700，total 900/2000），两个计时用例的 node:test 上限 5000 → 20000，HTTP 用例 idle 60 → 300、total 300 → 2000；③ `scripts/smoke-magpie-startup.mjs`：启动期限 100/300 → 750/1500，收进单一 `startupDeadlinesMs` 常量，报告里的 `deadlinesMs` 改为读该常量（原先写死 `[100, 300]`，改期限就会失真），elapsed 上界从固定 3000 改为 `timeout + 2000`；④ 同类且同样低于启动成本的 `test/mcp-subscriptions.test.ts:141`（100）、`test/mcp-logging.test.ts:85`（200）一并提到 2000。产品代码未改（S-7 不适用：改的是夹具预算，不是行为）。

**已收束（Q-094，2026-10-15）**：30 个 CPU 占用进程下（load 43-90）两文件全绿（lsp 16/16、mcp-progress 8/8；修复前同负载 lsp 15/16，第二次 14/16）；同一轮两个文件各 4 份并发副本全部 exit 0；startup 冒烟驱动在 load 37 下单跑 `startup passed`（三组、`deadlinesMs` 750/1500）；全量 `1341/1341`、`npm run check` 通过。证据 `research/ports/W45-load-budgets-evidence.json`。

## W179 clarification scroll and driver drift

The compiled 40-column, 10-row CLI reached the clarification index but scrolled to the preceding blank line. The action row remained below the viewport and the driver timed out. The focus offset now names the rendered action row. The new narrow test initially called a nonexistent public resize method; it now sends the output stream's existing resize event. The same narrow CLI path passes after that change. The existing dialog driver also still expected the pre-W175 review tab and pre-W176 preview-less answer; it now checks the source's immediate single-question submission and preview annotation. Evidence: `.audit/w179-cli-clarification.txt`, `.audit/w179-cli-dialogs.txt`, `.audit/w179-cli-preview.txt`. Independent review then reproduced loss of a current multi-select choice during clarification. The source f3 onChange [1158935,1159164) updates the answer immediately. The host now records choice and Other edits at that same boundary. A regression verifies clarification before submit. Causes: S-3 visible row coordinates, S-4 answer ownership, and S-6 consumer/driver synchronization.

## W180 native sidebar integration incidents

The first real mount exposed a hook-order failure. The source rxe memo calls U3t once, while the initial host U3t delegated to a hook-bearing sv getter. React error 311 appeared on the next native render. The host now reads the project context directly, retaining the source ordinary-getter contract. Initial source-copy starts omitted `var` twice; exact ranges were corrected and regenerated. Session fixtures also needed an explicit model and canonical macOS workspace path. The compiled driver initially treated pre-session grouping as absent data. Binding the panel before terminal.start fixes the owner start boundary, and the driver checks the explicit uncommitted base before inspecting hunks.

Independent review reproduced two P2 regressions. A separate sidebar chord resolver consumed the shared Ctrl+X prefix and cancelled the composer chord. The terminal now resolves all mounted contexts once, then invokes the registered native handler. Vim still read full terminal width after the composer narrowed. Its bridge now reads the same conversation width as painting and ordinary navigation. Both are covered in the existing sidebar regression. A second independent pass found cross-session queued attachments and selection retained after its native scope unmounted. Prompt attachments now carry the originating session ID and are checked at execution and restoration. The renderer clears selection whose scope no longer reaches its root. The existing native/sidebar regressions cover unmount/replacement and a queued /clear followed by an old-owner attachment. Native selection initially inferred only the empty Eg state and null followScroll frame. The host now declares their complete source-owned shapes. Causes are S-4 owner boundaries, S-6 reader synchronization and S-7 source getter semantics. Final validation passed 2598/2598, check/build and the compiled sidebar CLI. Both original later P2 probes passed independent verification after the fixes. The final content identity is e662b499f52b3c321d62112c4a98bdc73dc40af6aa5a048161563063dc55d8f5. See ports/W180-native-diff-panel-evidence.json.

## W59 source and compiler contract incidents

Unvalidated typed Zod placeholders and UUID template validation mixed static SDK types with runtime acceptance. Restore unknown/string runtime schemas and place SDK refinements at static export boundaries; UUID old/current acceptance was independently compared. Original-source digests were initially paired with HEAD-relative diff coordinates; bind both to the same original commit. Large original REPL diffs exceeded the default subprocess buffer; use a bounded 32 MiB capture. Remaining runtime bodies stay explicit compiler failures. See [W59](ports/W59-compile.md).

## W183 bounded repair fixture corrections

The shared-child independent persistence probe initially counted all root write results as written destinations. Five session updates correctly report skipped and one userSettings update reports written. The probe now selects the written destination before asserting ownership; the unchanged product passes. The coordinator replay initially used a data URL as a module base for absolute imports and failed before effects; stdin module evaluation restored the existing import contract.

The ordinary-fork content-twin fixture initially used a relative content-alias pattern where source Cf requires a rooted /content-alias pattern. Correcting that fixture exercises retained twins. The copied PLAN CLI driver's first generated replacement missed its old successful-write assertion; ENOENT showed the repaired product had already prevented the write. An exact driver edit changed only that assertion to absent-file, and the original 36-request chain passed at identity6b778562. Causes are S-1 fixture diagnosis and S-5/S-6 explicit assertion/driver contracts. Product semantics were not changed to accommodate these fixture errors.

W183 auto integration exposed an actual rule-representation defect. The recovered Un parser maps Bash(*) to bare Bash, while a startup bucket retained raw Bash(*). The copied nz/gDs reducer serialized the removal as Bash, so it did not remove the raw entry. The failing PLAN assertion at agent-lifecycle1169 was read before repair. The host refresh boundary now canonicalizes allow rules before invoking the copied transform, and the same existing group verifies strip, false-setting restore and nested root ownership; affected-final4 passes131/131. This is S-1/S-6/S-7 representation-boundary work, not a relaxed expected value. A separate long private TMPDIR made a fixed4096 child-journal fixture reject metadata before its intended large-response path. The fixture accounts for path bytes while retaining the journal size/lock assertions and now asserts boundedCalls===1 to prove an actual provider call reached that stage.

## W310 cron watcher and owner corrections

The W309 baseline reconstructed the entire durable file from its in-memory job list. A source-first probe replaced a file row externally and then created another job. The external row was lost and accepted metadata was stripped. Source Pot, s_e and bds instead mutate the latest file. W310 uses those append/remove/stamp bodies and orders its own mutations and watcher reloads on one queue. Independent owner tracing also found that initial schema construction and the actual session each started a scheduler with the same PID lock identity. The initial owner could consume catch-up before the real session. Schema construction now stays passive, the actual session owns its lock, and runtime cleanup awaits an in-flight attach and every pending write. These are S-4 and S-7 corrections. Independent review then reproduced a recurring fire delivered twice on lease handoff. The waiting scheduler had cached a deadline before the holder stamped its fire. On acquisition it retained that deadline even after reading the newer lastFiredAt. Recovered ne calls ke before filling the due cache. Acquisition now discards unowned file deadlines and rebuilds them from the latest file. The existing lease regression now makes the holder fire before handoff and checks that the waiter waits for the following occurrence.

The first unlink regression deleted the file immediately after successive atomic writes while the recovered ww watcher was still collecting a stable write. Both the raw recovered watcher and the port produced no unlink callback in that sequence. The regression now verifies unlink after the observed stable reload, and the package retains the rapid-edit limitation. A CLI probe separately froze Date.now, preventing the watcher's 300 ms stability timer from finishing. Its controlled clock now advances with real elapsed time between explicit schedule jumps. Neither failure was fixed by changing the product's source timing. These are S-1 and S-5 fixture corrections. The first ad-hoc event probe also waited for a missing parent directory to become watchable; seeding the owned directory isolated the intended existing-file event sequence. All owned probe processes were stopped or exited.

## W311 creator predicate and fixture corrections

The baseline at 222c0e9 held the scheduler lease and fired a task whose foreign creator PID and birth token were still live. Source ke [5987,6337) requires creator eligibility before ne fills a deadline. W311 ports that branch rather than extending lease ownership as a substitute. The first typecheck found that filtering an optional session ID with a compound predicate did not narrow it to string under this compiler. The transcript lookup now narrows explicitly before the recovered UUID check. The first affected run passed the three new creator groups and failed an old handoff expectation only on ordering. Its fixture had changed from create-after-attach to a creatorless file row loaded before the local job; equal deadlines and creation stamps therefore retain file-first insertion order. The literal expectation now follows that fixture while preserving the no-duplicate assertion. Source behavior was unchanged for that correction. These incidents fall under S-1, S-2, S-4 and S-7.

The first compiled creator chain reached the restart and then exited with `Resume uses stored settings; start a new session to change them`. The new launch helper incorrectly forwarded `--user-dir` together with `--resume`, contrary to src/cli.ts:605-606 and the existing smoke-coding-task driver. The fixture now omits that setting on resume and uses the pinned journal configuration. The complete compiled creator chain then passes with six owned loopback requests and released locks. This is an S-1/S-6 driver correction; the product's resume validation stayed unchanged.

The first CLI test command named `test/cli-background.test.ts`, which does not exist. Node ran the other three files and reported 25 passing tests. A focused path inventory identified `test/background-session.test.ts`; that actual suite then passed 6/6. The evidence now distinguishes 25 CLI tests plus six background-session tests rather than treating the initial command as complete coverage. This is S-1/S-6 verification-path drift.

Independent W311 review paused the first task-file read after a contended scheduler acquired the lease. The port removed only its eligibility marker, published held and left the old lastFiredAt in the list projection. A timer tick then rebuilt a due time from that stale projection and duplicated the holder's just-persisted fire. The bounded probe confirmed equal dueAt/firedAt and cleaned up. Source ne calls ke before b; the W310 protection had removed the entire unowned job before publication. W311 now removes both the ineligible job projection and its deadline marker before publishing held, while retaining valid current/prior creator jobs. The existing handoff group pauses the same read and asserts that tick emits nothing during that gap. This is S-4/S-7 lifecycle ownership, not a timer-delay adjustment. Once the fix restored W310's projection removal, equal-time iteration again followed local/local/file order. The first repaired run had 99/100 with only that literal fixture ordering mismatch; the expectation now matches the restored insertion order and the next affected run passes 100/100. A fresh independent reviewer then ran the paused-read handoff test once, passed 1/1 and confirmed the P1 closed with timer/lock cleanup.

## W330 source and fixture preflight

A resumed source-read one-liner had one excess array bracket and failed before reading source. The corrected command verifies the three module hashes and reads only the acquired UTF-16 ranges. A coding-standard read exceeded the text budget because many historical rows are long; the next read uses40lines. Guessed commands.ts, line-terminal.ts and smoke-mcp-management-cli.mjs paths were absent; focused inventories locate interactive.ts, terminal.ts/tui.ts and the existing .audit/w300 driver. These are S-1 evidence-location corrections with no product behavior inferred from failure. apply_patch remains absent (command-v exit1), so dedicated Write/Edit is the documented fallback.

The first new status run passed7/8 and failed only the piped fixture's literal default gateway/counting expectation. defaultConfig legitimately reads this shell's gateway environment, so the fixture accidentally expected provider counting and an omitted gateway. It now pins an owned loopback gateway and estimate counting explicitly, retaining the full JSON equality assertion. Product headless serialization is unchanged. This is an S-1/S-5 fixture input correction.

The first changed compiled /status shows `[38;2;...m` literals because the generic dialog removes ESC from a colored message. The pure string test had missed the real display boundary. Reading renderDialog and source Ye/j establishes that Status strings must reach native Text. The repair carries plain value text plus typed semantic spans through statusRows, keeps normal message cleaning, and lets the mounted native theme own color. The initial palette getter is removed. The same existing first regression group now opens a real TuiTerminal prompt, decodes xterm cells and checks light ANSI plus live dark repaint. This is S-3/S-6 adapter evidence, not an ANSI-strip assertion workaround. Two guessed dialog filenames were absent; the existing owner is tui.ts. One exact Edit found two message-field matches and changed nothing; the next anchors on titleOrigin. The final record-check one-liner repeated the delimiter typo class with one surplus parenthesis before any check ran. Replace that ad-hoc one-liner with the small w330-final-record-check.mjs file;it checks complete identity arrays and all recorded target ranges/body hashes. Product files remain unchanged by that verifier correction.

## W312 loading evidence and teammate scope

Source preflight showed that agentId cron targets a separate teammate context and task registry. The existing local AgentRegistry has ordinary isolated children and explicitly removes scheduler tools. The work package therefore records that dependency and ports the independently ready ne loading guard instead of inventing teammate semantics. The ec60673 baseline fired/stamped a durable recurring task after three busy minutes because no loading callback was read. The port now reads actual Agent.busy through session-owned runtime readers and checks on idle action completion; held compiled requests prove the file stays unchanged until delivery.

The first focused source searches named nonexistent host files (`subagent.ts`, `commands.ts`, `runtime-abilities.ts`) and a broad queue projection returned excessive history. Subsequent reads used the actual plural subagents.ts, existing runtime handlers and exact bounded row/AST ranges. No behavior was inferred from those failed searches. Independent trail review also found missing direct check/build log pointers on an otherwise valid verification row; an append-only evidence row supplies both actual paths. These are S-1 evidence-location corrections. No product failure or source-timing adjustment was required; independent idle tests pass3/3.

W332 review transport (2026-10-25). Three independent reviewers were dispatched for the same five correctness questions and all three died on upstream transport errors (two ECONNRESET, one 502) with no verdict. None is recorded as passing and their partial readings are discarded; the package status stays BLOCKED for independent review while a fourth minimal-prompt attempt runs. A consolidated single-file review packet was built so a reviewer needs one read instead of a multi-file tour, which reduced the exposure but did not remove it. The main thread ran the same five questions itself and recorded the result explicitly as self-review, not as an independent verdict. No product change follows from this; the rule this class of incident needs is already covered by the existing independent-review gate, so no new coding-standard rule is added.

## W385 outer permission range boundary

A dependency scan of `$pn` [3360777,3364305) failed to parse its `await`. The range began at `function`, dropping the preceding `async ` token. Acorn reports the complete declaration at [3360771,3364305), SHA-256 `c2d85e5e158c95b150ff8c9a15e381da445ab4f694a044754aa168f1b95d208a`; the earlier shorter hash remains a historical slice rather than a complete-function coordinate. Future source copies and dependency scans must use the full AST range. This is an S-1/S-7 range-boundary correction.

## W385 failure-classifier factory signature

After adding the copied `G9e` classifier, six focused tests passed but TypeScript reported five old factory calls missing its new fourth argument. The adapter now defaults the optional dependency container to an empty object, so existing `Zwe`/`Dpn` fixture calls retain their three-argument signature; `G9e` callers still supply error classes and predicates. The copied source bodies are unchanged. This is an S-1/S-6 adapter signature correction.

## W385 persistent parser failure scope

The scheduler abort test originally injected a throwing parser while its `MTr` was a stub. Source `MTr` invokes the copied `Kg` when deny rules exist; `Kg` calls the parser directly and the same persistent injected error propagates there. The earlier scheduler ask therefore establishes the shared Symbol boundary only under its explicit `MTr` fixture, not the behavior of every complete source-policy dependency. Keep this exception boundary in the package record and test it with the real copied `MTr` before claiming full-chain coverage. This is an S-1/S-5/S-7 observation-scope correction.

## W385 complex-policy fixture narrowing

The new complex-policy behavioral tests passed 3/3, but the initial TypeScript check failed because `assert.deepEqual(calls, [])` narrowed the captured callback array to `never[]` before a later callback pushed a string. The assertion now checks the zero length directly, retaining the same observation without changing source code. This repeats the W385 test-narrowing class already covered by S-1/S-2.

## W385 shared parser-abort state

A scheduler → vector → command-policy fixture with a 10,001-unit input failed with `TypeError: e.children is not iterable`. The independently copied analyser and clamp both constructed the same source `Yx` declaration, so the parser's Symbol differed from the analyser's Symbol despite identical declarations. The recovered module owns one `Yx` shared by `w6` and `E6`; use the analyser's existing copied singleton in the clamp and retain a full-chain regression for the source `PARSE_ABORT` approval result. This is an S-4/S-7 source-state ownership defect. Earlier clamp abort tests did not expose it because `fTr` rejects its own parser Symbol before calling the analyser.

## W385 clamp rewrite baseline

A child with `Bash(echo:*)` executed the command supplied by a PermissionRequest hook after the hook replaced an allowed `echo` input with a `printf` input. The focused child-registry test failed with `[run_command: exit code 0]`; its disposable workspace was removed. Source `sxr` rechecks updated input through `yk`, which reaches the shell permission check including `O3e` and its `fTr` clamp. The host only repeated ordinary `permissionDecision`. Keep the failing regression as the baseline and connect the copied clamp at both checks after its independent review. Two further child-registry controls failed as expected at the old gate: a denied compound reported the entire line instead of the source's `printf forbidden` span, and `command echo clamped-ok` was denied instead of matching the source's unwrapped candidate. These are S-1/S-7 execution-path observations, not test expectation changes. The first compiled workflow-driver attempt ended before a child ran: `workflow-script` normalized a flat source rule list to `string[][]`, then `AgentRegistry.launch` validated that nested array as if it were a flat external list. Source `chunk-hedqf2m1.js` [46284,47451) keeps the normalized rules flat until the permission layer is appended. Restore that intermediate shape and retain per-spawn grouping at the child boundary. The first driver error lacked the workflow receipt; it now prints that receipt on a missing child result. After the flat-list repair and build, the compiled workflow started the child and reached the expected old-gate failure for `command echo clamped-ok; printf second`: the entire line was reported unmatched despite both source-matched segments. This is an S-1/S-6 cross-boundary data-shape defect and a confirmed compiled CLI baseline. A separate test makes the second invocation of the copied parser throw after its initial parse; the current gate never invokes that parser and runs the echo. The test now passes with the recovered `sor` fail-closed message after source integration. The first integration typecheck reported a widened copied-JavaScript `kind` and the parser's two-argument inferred signature. The host now narrows the copied result at its boundary and the injected test forwards the optional deadline; neither copied body was altered. The source-scope scanner has no `--help` flag and treated it as an input filename; its header documents the positional path interface. No source file changed during that failed lookup. Invoking the copy-plan generator with no arguments printed its documented positional usage and exited before writing; use `PLAN MODULE_DIRECTORY OUTPUT_DIRECTORY` for the next generation.

The first research-only patch used a paragraph from the package record as a CURRENT anchor and failed atomically. Rechecked the actual headings and applied the bounded update. Cause: record/anchor mismatch; no product files were changed.

The first check reported `event.prompt` may be undefined in the new consumer regression. Cause: `RuntimeEvent` includes non-notification variants. The assertion now narrows `event.kind` before reading `prompt`; focused test, check and build pass. Classification: test union type narrowing.

# W385 record patch context (2026-11-01)

Two attempts to insert the stop task card failed because the patch expected a partial line, while the existing paragraph is a single long line. The card was inserted using an exact existing line. Classification: patch context selection; no product files changed by the failed attempts.

# W385 wake record patch context (2026-11-01)
